The background backlog is downloads plus your own music folders. On iPhone and iPad it only runs on power with Sounding open (`AnalysisLanePolicy.allowsBacklog`); a fresh download or a played track still analyses on battery as before. Tracks over 20 minutes are never analysed in the background.
  - **Tracks on the decks** (owner, 2026-11-12) are analysed on every device that mixes, Apple TV included, past the power, battery and playing gates (Music Analysis off still stops it) and while music plays (that's the point): tempo, beats and bars, key, energy and intro, from the audio the deck already holds, never a second download. Results land in the same analysis cache (synced as usual) and in the deck readout, and Auto Mix re-plans while the mix point is still more than 45 s away. Apple TV runs one analysis at a time at utility priority and pauses while a deck loads or a blend is running, so playback never drops out. Its library still gets BPM and key from other devices; it never analyses a backlog. While a deck's analysis runs the readout says it's analysing; an unknown value is never shown as a guess (no estimated 50% energy).
  - **Empty BPM or key** is never blank: a small `clock` in tertiary when it will be found here, a `—` when it won't (not downloaded, nothing found), a spinner while queued or analysing; the help tag and the accessibility label say why, from the gate. Column rows show `—`.
  - **Analyse Now** (track menu and Tempo & Key) runs at once on this device on the real pipeline, ignoring the gate; only tracks with audio on this device (downloads and your own files) offer it. **Analyse All Now** (Settings › Music Analysis, under "BPM and Key", with the count) ignores the power, playing and battery gate until done or Stop, and stops on thermal serious or critical with "Stopped: your Mac is too warm." Nothing extra is persisted; progress is read from the analysis cache.
  - **Manual values**: BPM 40–250 with decimals and Tap Tempo (four taps or more, the average of the last eight, a 2 s pause starts again), key from the 24 Camelot keys shown with their musical names ("8A · A minor"), and genre. They're stored as the existing `sounding.userCorrection` analysis row, which outranks measured values everywhere (sorting, columns, Auto Mix, the DJ planner and transitions) and syncs with the rest of the analysis cache over iCloud; no new CloudKit record type or field. **Reset to Analysed** writes an empty, newer correction row (keeping a corrected genre) rather than deleting, so the reset syncs too. Set values carry a small `pencil` mark ("Set by you"). A manual BPM that differs from the measured grid replaces it with a tempo-only grid anchored on the first downbeat; a locked Prep grid still wins. The Mac table edits through the sheet (double-click plays, so cells aren't edited inline).
- **Analysis vs rekordbox** (owner, 2026-11-09: "compare analysis quality of rekordbox to Sounding"). `RekordboxComparisonTests` in the `SoundingTVMacTests` target (Mac-hosted, `SoundingTV macOS` scheme) reads the owner's local rekordbox ANLZ files read-only (`.DAT`/`.EXT` only, never a `.db`), finds each audio file with the import's matcher, runs the production audio tier into a temporary cache (never the real one) and writes `rekordbox-comparison.md` and `.csv`: BPM classes, beat offset and drift, downbeats and bar phase, variable tempo, phrase F-measure at ±1 beat and ±1 bar, intro and outro, key (only with a rekordbox XML), cues and speed, by genre and BPM range, with the 15 worst per metric. It skips unless `SOUNDING_COMPARE_REKORDBOX=1`. Env: `COMPARE_OUT`, `COMPARE_LIMIT`, `COMPARE_CONCURRENCY`, `COMPARE_BEATTRACKER=0`, `COMPARE_MUSIC_DIRS` (default `~/Music`), `REKORDBOX_XML`, `REKORDBOX_ANLZ_ROOT`; prefix each with `TEST_RUNNER_` for `xcodebuild`. The sandboxed host needs `SOUNDING_MAC_ENTITLEMENTS=Tests/SoundingTVTests/CompareHost.entitlements` (Debug only, read-only file access plus `/private/tmp`). Run: `TEST_RUNNER_SOUNDING_COMPARE_REKORDBOX=1 TEST_RUNNER_COMPARE_OUT=/tmp/sounding-vs-rekordbox xcodebuild test -project SoundingTV.xcodeproj -scheme "SoundingTV macOS" -destination 'platform=macOS' -only-testing:SoundingTVMacTests/RekordboxComparisonTests/testCompareAnalysisWithRekordbox SOUNDING_MAC_ENTITLEMENTS=Tests/SoundingTVTests/CompareHost.entitlements SWIFT_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL=-O`.
- **Testing etiquette: real auth only** (owner, 2026-11-09: "no more fixtures/fake logins. we only use real auth/real accounts now"). Every device, simulator, UI test, drive and screenshot uses the real signed-in account; never `SOUNDING_TV_UI_FIXTURE` or a fake session. The owner signs each simulator in once, or the session is moved with the app's own link flow. Agents never type credentials, sign out, or read, print or log tokens or keychain items. Real traffic counts against one account, so keep signed-in simulators few, space launches out, and stop at the first 429. Apple TV navigation (the album page, Decks, every tab's focus and Menu path, Settings sub-pages) is checked by `TVNavigationTests` on the real Apple TV; a test that has to start playback to reach a screen pauses it at once.

---

## 10. The website (sounding.forbes.red, `docs/`)
- **Source:** the site is built in the lab at `sounding-site-lab/v2` and published by copying it into `docs/` (live since 3f7405f8, 12 Nov 2026). It replaced the scroll-told HTML site and its `site.js` scene engine.
- **Homepage:** one WebGL2 scene (no libraries), driven by scroll, with damped scroll and soft "stations" where the camera holds so the page never feels jumpy.
  - **Opening:** "Plug in. All the way." A long aux lead with its jack, decks and a mixer on a table. Scroll, or "push the jack down", and the jack plugs in. Records can be dragged and knobs turned, playing a beat made for the page. "Unplug" undoes it.
  - **Two sides:** a Listening ↔ DJing slider in the header crossfades the page between day (listening) and night (DJing).
    - Listening: playback, Likes, a long set and its tracklist, Play On to the TV, the Watch as remote, SharePlay and Auto Mix.
    - DJing: the Mac decks, cues, rekordbox, Prep, analysis, USB export and the CDJs.
  - **Devices:** iPhone, a friend's iPhone, iPad, Mac, Apple TV and Apple Watch. Their screens are drawn from the real app (`screens.js`), redrawn at display resolution so they stay sharp on Retina. The TV's sign-in screen matches the app: dark, "Plug in. Turn it up.", the QR plate, "<device> is nearby: open Sounding on it to sign in." and Play Sound with its orange pulse.
  - **Music:** the owner's real music. The featured set is Essy at Wildeburg 2025, with its real tracklist. Likes lead with Kyle Starkey, Pegassi, Benwal and HearThuG.
  - **Text sits over the canvas:** selectable and clickable while the canvas stays interactive. The header text switches between dark and light ink against whatever is behind it, never on a blurred backing.
  - **Order:** hero and DJ teaser; Listening (Likes, set, tracklist, rekordbox, Prep, USB); phone to TV and cues; Auto Mix and two decks; "Fair warning."; footer.
- **Other pages:**
  - Apple TV (`docs/tv/`);
  - Features (`docs/features/`), listing every feature worth mentioning by platform, with unbuilt ones under "Maybe. I will if I want.";
  - DJ (`docs/dj/`), privacy and support.
- **Copy is the owner's, word for word.** The Fair warning, the footer and any line the owner writes go in exactly as written, typos included ("dissapointments", "greatful", "realiably", "the reals deals", "endorsed by me"), with no spelling, casing or quote fixes. Everything else is in the voice of the Fair warning: dry, British, first person, jokes in passing, "tbh" allowed.
- **Footer:** "Mused lovingly in Amsterdam, 🇳🇱" then "Despite their many attempts, SoundCloud is not affiliated with or endorsed by me."
- **Call to action:** none. The site is invite only and never mentions TestFlight or an App Store release beyond the Fair warning's joke.
- **Plans:**
  - Say plainly that a free account gets SoundCloud's previews and full tracks need Go or Go+.
  - Never say that free accounts play SoundCloud tracks in full.
  - Never mention the personal-only SoundCloud audio export.
- **Claims are checked against the code** before they go on the site. Some examples:
  - The Watch's crown sets the volume when it's controlling a TV, and scrubs when the phone is playing.
  - The DDJ-400 is the only mapped controller.
  - Export to USB (⇧⌘E) exists in Prep, but hasn't met a real CDJ.
  - CarPlay isn't mentioned until Apple grants the entitlement.
- **Technical:**
  - static HTML/CSS/JS, no build step, no external requests;
  - every page is `noindex, nofollow`, and `robots.txt` has `Disallow: /`;
  - reduced motion is honoured;
  - **Never modify `docs/auth/` or `docs/CNAME`.**
- **Publishing:**
  - a docs-only commit to `main`, copied from the lab;
  - start from the current `docs/` on main;
  - never push the app branch to publish the site.

## 11. Engineering rules that affect design
- **No code comments,** except an occasional `// MARK:`.
- **Never run xcodegen.** The `.pbxproj` is hand-maintained; register new files for every target that compiles them.
- **On-disk stores take their folder from `AppDataDirectory`** (`Core/Support/AppDataDirectory.swift`): Application Support everywhere except tvOS, which only keeps Caches; the folder is created on first use and falls back to Caches, then the temporary directory. Never build a store path from `FileManager.urls(for:)` directly.
- **Where data lives** (owner, 2026-11-09: "let me pick the location…", then refined: catalogue in iCloud, heavy data in a hidden folder). Three kinds, nothing else:
  - **Catalogue** (library index, Likes, playlists, own-music catalogue, prep and cues) stays in its local database and syncs through the existing iCloud paths (`LibraryChunk`, cue sync, synced settings). No folder picking. Its formats are already compact and merge-safe: LZFSE chunks bucketed by hash with writer and time, per-cue last-writer-wins with tombstones.
  - **Sounding Data** (`SoundingDataLocation`, `Core/Support/SoundingData*.swift`): analysis (BPM, key, grids, features, waveforms), insights, mix history, Auto Mix ratings and setlists. It lives in Application Support/Sounding Data (Caches on Apple TV), hidden. Settings › Downloads shows its size on iPhone, iPad and Mac (`SoundingDataSettingsSection`); the Mac adds Show in Finder and, as a secondary control, Change Location… (a security-scoped bookmark; Move, or Use Existing / Merge when the folder already has Sounding Data; Use Default Location… moves it back or leaves it). If the chosen folder goes away, writes go to Sounding Data (Offline) with a one-line notice, and are moved in when it returns. Apple TV and Apple Watch keep no location setting.
  - **Machine-local** stays in Application Support or Caches and never moves: SoundCloud downloads and anything encrypted or tied to the account or device (a SoundCloud terms rule), artwork, lyrics, the network log and traffic ledger, the Spotlight ledger, AI model weights, the DJ transition cache, and the analysis SQLite index.
- **Sounding Data is cloud-safe** so it can sit on iCloud Drive, Dropbox, Google Drive or a NAS with two devices writing:
  - no live databases, lock files or in-place rewrites in the folder; SQLite stays outside as a rebuildable index (the analysis index keeps a ledger of files it has read and re-reads only changed ones);
  - every file is `SoundingDataFormat`: a 16-byte little-endian header (`SNDG`, format version, kind, flags, raw length), then packed JSON (varints, Float16 for arrays of 64+ values within ±2, Float32 where it is within 0.1 ms, UInt8 and integer arrays lossless, Float64 otherwise), LZFSE through the Compression framework when smaller;
  - analysis is one immutable-ish file per track and engine, sharded (`Analysis/ab/<track hash>/<engine>-<key hash>.snda`); setlists one file per track (`.snds`); insights, mix history and ratings are per-device journals (`<area>/<device id>/<time>-<seq>.sndj`) that each device only appends to and compacts in its own folder, merged on read by last writer (time, then device, then sequence) with tombstones and clear markers;
  - writes are atomic (temp file, then rename) through `NSFileCoordinator`, skipped when the bytes are unchanged, and batched (analysis 2 s, journals 1 s); reads are memory-mapped; an `NSFilePresenter` reloads on outside changes;
  - evicted iCloud files start downloading and are read on the next pass; conflict copies ("x 2", "(conflicted copy)", `NSFileVersion`) merge deterministically (analysis: newest `producedAt`, then producer; setlists: `SetlistMerging`; journals: union) and are deleted;
  - old formats migrate once: the analysis index exports its rows, and the old JSON files are imported and renamed `.migrated`, never deleted. Clear Cache keeps your BPM, key and genre corrections.
- **`DeviceClass`** is the one device-identity enum. **`TokenTransferProtocol.swift`** stays dependency-free (it compiles into the watch, Share and widget targets).
- **ShazamKit is not an entitlement.** Never add `com.apple.developer.shazamkit` to an `.entitlements` file; it's enabled through App Services on the App ID. Microphone strings (`NSMicrophoneUsageDescription`) on iPhone, Mac and Watch name both uses: identifying a song with Shazam and hearing the Apple TV's chime. Lyrics come from LRCLIB (decided 2026-11-08).
- **Keychain goes through `SoundingKeychain`** (`Core/Keychain/SoundingKeychain.swift`; owner, 2026-11-09: "i see too many of these" about the Mac's login keychain password prompt). No direct `SecItem*` calls. On the Mac every item lives in the data protection keychain, so a rebuilt or differently signed Sounding never asks for the login password. An item still in the old login keychain moves over once on first read: copied, read back, then the old one deleted. If that read is refused, nothing new is created in its place (no new vault key, no sign-out); it stays pending and is retried next launch. XCTest hosts and unsigned or ad-hoc builds use an in-memory keychain. Snapshot harness runs read the real data protection keychain but never write to it. Neither mints vault keys.
- **Share screens and logic across platforms** (owner, 2026-11-09: "the more screens and logic we share the better"). Put views, view models and logic in `SoundingTV.swiftpm/Sources/Shared/` or Core and use them on every platform. Keep per-platform code to genuine idiom differences such as chrome, focus, sidebars and input. Before writing a platform-specific copy of a screen or state, look for a shared one and extend it. When touching duplicated code, merge it. Infrastructure is never device-specific (owner, 2026-11-12: "these things MUST be there for all devices"): caching, performance fixes, networking and data features go in shared code and are wired up on every app in the same change. iOS 27-only APIs keep a working iOS 26 path, because the owner's iPad runs iPadOS 26.
- **One component per pattern** (owner, 2026-11-09: "share components so we can reuse, such as tables… album hero (to use for artists/profile)"). Every track list uses the one shared track list (one row style with optional columns, the Mac table), and every collection or person header uses the one shared hero: album, playlist, set, artist, profile and other users. Extend these components; never build a one-off copy. The components, all in `Shared/`:
  - **`CollectionHero`** (`CollectionHeroHeader`, `CollectionHeroPlayButtons`) for albums, playlists and sets: artwork, title, subtitle link, metadata line, description, Play and Shuffle. Layouts: stacked (iPhone), leading (iPad, Mac, TV), slim (above the iPad table).
  - **`PersonHero`** (`PersonHeroImage`, `PersonHeroTitle`, `PersonHeroPlayButton`) for artists, profiles and other users. The image fills only its own frame (never an unclipped `.fill` that spills sideways), and pages ignore only the top safe area, so on iPad and Mac the hero starts at the detail column's edge, never under the sidebar. Over the image the name and badge are always white with the bottom scrim and a soft shadow, whatever the image or appearance; while the hero is under the bar the toolbar uses `.toolbarColorScheme(.dark)`, scoped to that page; pinned in the bar the name is the normal title colour.
  - **`CollectionAccent`** never leaks: `.collectionAccent(from:)` only puts the accent in the environment; hero buttons (`collectionAccentTint()`) and the playing mark (`collectionAccentTinted()`) apply it locally. Never `.tint` a whole page (it reaches the shared navigation bar) and never use `.preferredColorScheme` on a pushed page or tab (it darkens the whole window); it belongs only on a full-screen presentation.
  - **`TrackColumnLayout`** (`Shared/ViewModels/TrackTableColumns.swift`) is the one column setting (`trackList.columns.v1`): Title always first and on, then the user's order and choice of Artist, BPM, Key, Genre, Duration, Date Added, Album or Release, Plays, Likes, Energy, Released and Source. Both Mac tables read it; iPhone and iPad column rows use the fixed Title, Artist, Album, Time, BPM and Key set. Edited in Settings › Library › Columns (iPhone and iPad, Edit to reorder, Reset to Default) and the Mac Library settings tab and table header menus.
  - **`SCTrackListRow`** with `SCTrackListItem` and `SCTrackListMoreMenu`: number or artwork, title, artist, duration or lock, the ••• menu, and the playing mark. iOS `TrackRow`, the Mac `MacTrackRows` and the TV album and artist lists all draw it.
  - **`CollectionShelf`** and **`ArtworkShelfCard`** for the shelves under a page, fed by `CollectionRelatedModel`.
  - **`CollectionAccent`** for the page colour (§3).
- **Personal-build code** lives only in `SoundingTV-macOS/Sources/Personal/` inside `#if SOUNDING_PERSONAL`, compiled only by the `Personal` configuration. Never reference it from shared code without the same `#if`. `release.sh` refuses any binary carrying `SoundingPersonalMarker`.

---

## 12. Process (for agents)
- **Agents write code only** (owner, 2026-11-09: "sub agents for all of these just write code, do not test or build that happens here"). Subagents never build, run tests, or use simulators or devices. The main session merges, builds every platform, runs the tests and does all device checks.
- **Read this file first.** Brief every subagent with it, linking this file and quoting the relevant sections.
- **Model routing** (user's rule): no Fable. Opus for engine/architecture and hard UI (rebuilds, motion). Sonnet at high effort for checks, audits and routine UI fixes. Always set model and effort.
- **Verification is mandatory** before claiming done:
  - screenshots in light, dark and accessibility XL, of the **real states users hit**: scrolled pages with full-bleed artwork under the toolbar, pushed pages, empty, loading and error states;
  - look at them, compare against the prototypes, and measure CPU/hitches when you touch motion;
  - a reviewer approving from two screenshots isn't verification.
- **Simulators:** use the shared pool only (iPhones 3E6A3345…, 342BE016…; TV 84BB9394…). Don't create new ones.
- **Real devices run TestFlight builds only** (owner, 2026-11-12). Xcode-signed builds use the Development CloudKit database, so they strand the shared SoundCloud session. Install Xcode builds on simulators; read device logs with `devicectl device process launch --console`. The Apple TV and iPad are approved for testing. The iPhone only for Action button, StandBy and alarm tests. Ask the user to sign in on a device; never work around sign-in.
- **Mac app:** kill only by PID, never `pkill -x Sounding`.
- **Screenshot drives open pages by link** (Release-safe, iPhone, iPad and Mac): `soundingtv://open-page?url=<SoundCloud https URL>` resolves the link and navigates without playing: an artist or playlist opens on Home (iPad: a playlist with its own sidebar item opens there), a track opens Behind the Track (`SoundingPageLinkRouter` in `WidgetLinkRouter.swift`). Only soundcloud.com hosts are accepted.
- **User messages:** a new message during work means "also", not "cancel".
- **Matrix:** update the rows in `Design/MATRIX.md` for what you touched (status, date, note).

The personality is how adventurous it is: Wedding DJ blends, echoes out and cuts; Auto Mix adds breakdown exits, drop-ins and filter or echo builds in the last 30% of a track; Junkie adds loop builds (two an hour), reverb washes, tempo ramps, bridges and jumps. Rails: every landing is on a measured downbeat, no move without measured downbeats on both tracks (otherwise a cut on the bar), no blend over 4 bars across a key clash, no vocal overlap, Skip and Pause always win, a seek during a loop build cancels it. **Energy** (Auto Mix settings): Follow the Night, a Chill–Peak slider, and **Match My Workout**: heart rate from Apple Watch through HealthKit, primed by a centred "Match Your Workout" screen before the system prompt, forwarded from the iPhone to the Apple TV when the TV is the Output; on the Watch it starts a workout. When either track's vocals are unknown, the incoming plays with its mids 9 dB down until the outgoing has gone. AVPlayer playback is kept in step by `AVPlaybackCoordinator`; play, pause, seek and track changes also travel as stamped messages (newest stamp wins) for the cases it doesn't cover. **Auto Mix is off during SharePlay** and comes back when the session ends: the mix engine isn't an AVPlayer, so the coordinator can't hold it, and each device would plan its own blends from its own analysis, so mixes would land at different moments; a shared mix clock is option B and wasn't chosen. Joining costs no SoundCloud requests on anyone's device: one participant answers the newcomer with the state it already has, and the newcomer resolves only the track it plays, as for any play. The Output picker becomes the **Connect sheet**: current Output on top (name, quality badge, what's playing, volume), every other Output, Sonos groups with ••• to group and ungroup, then Bluetooth & AirPlay. iPhone: mini player Play On… and Now Playing Output; Mac: the toolbar Play On popover; Apple TV: Sonos rows in the Output panel. The footer says the device must stay on the Wi-Fi (`Design/specs/devices-and-auth.md` §5.9). |

The time-stretcher searches a full pitch period for its splice on sustained material and crossfades at constant power using the measured correlation, so a held pad keeps its level. Blend dips are gated on the engine-caused part only: each audible deck's own departure from its body level is the music, not the mix. |
| 2026-11-07 | **Auto Mix: vocals, runway and peaks.** The TV's own analysis counts a weak but steady voice held for 16 s or more as a vocal, so the payoff and vocal rails work there as they do with full Music Analysis. When both tracks have 16 or more vocal-free bars at the seam, Auto Mix keeps the long bass-swap blend instead of an FX build (Adventurous excepted). If no mix passes, it steps down to an echo out or a short blend at the outgoing's end before a clean cut. The master limiter catches inter-sample peaks (true peak ≤ −1 dBTP) by easing the gain down ahead of them, never by clipping. |

| 2026-11-09 | **Auto Mix level and band ownership (engine, wave 1 lane B).** Each transition measures short-term K-weighted (BS.1770) loudness per bar from the PCM already decoded on both decks (8 bars either side of the outgoing exit, 16 from the incoming entry) and sets bounded static trims so the two steady bodies match within 1 LU: boost the incoming only within its peak headroom (to −1 dBTP less 0.5 dB, at most +6 dB), otherwise lower the louder side (incoming up to −9 dB, outgoing up to −4 dB over the first bars). Whole-track normalisation now also boosts quiet tracks within headroom when the peak is known, with a −14 dB floor. The fader law is no longer "both decks at unity at the midpoint": the crossfade curve is chosen per transition from both decks' band shares and the planned EQ (bass kill, mid handover, filters) so the predicted sum stays flat (dip costs twice a swell). When both tracks have busy mids, the incoming mids rise from −6 dB to 0 by the bass swap while the outgoing mids fall to −6 dB after it. Filter shape is a planner choice per move: plain, high-pass (outgoing), low-pass (incoming opening), combined, or filter out (the loop move's HPF 900 Hz with mids and highs to −40 dB, still the loop default until the planner chooses). Loop and seek seams can no longer add more than +3 dB or exceed either source's sample. Cancelling a mid-track move ramps the controls that move owns back to neutral (filters, EQ, sends, resonance) or to their earlier value (gain, rate), clears its loop and returns to the dry position; user-held controls are never touched. |
| 2026-11-09 | **SoundCloud refresh ownership.** Refresh-token rotation means a shared pair has one live refresh token. Every device takes part in session sync (the Mac too), compares pairs by issue time and generation rather than when it noticed them, adopts a newer pair before refreshing, never resends a refused refresh token, and holds authenticated requests while the sign-in needs reconnecting. Rules in §9. |
| 2026-11-09 | **rekordbox without its database** (owner decision; `Design/specs/rekordbox-parity.md`). Sounding never opens `master.db` or the `.edb` files and never adds SQLCipher, permanently. The import routes are rekordbox's plain ANLZ analysis files and its collection XML. Mac Settings › Library › rekordbox › **Import Analysis…** ("Import from rekordbox") reads `~/Library/Pioneer/rekordbox/share/PIONEER/USBANLZ` after the user picks the rekordbox folder (read-only bookmark, `O_RDONLY` reads, `.3EX` never opened, nothing written). It matches by file name and duration, because rekordbox anonymises the path to `?/<file>` there, then by XML location, then by title. Grids and phrases come in with the `rekordbox` source, and cues with the `imported` origin. User and locked values win. Phrases keep rekordbox's own kind and mood, so a USB export writes the same PSSI back. Tracks with no phrases get PSSI from Sounding's cached arrangement. One line in the sheet tells the user to export the collection XML from rekordbox for cue points, keys and playlists, because rekordbox 7 keeps cues out of the local ANLZ files. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Import a whole rekordbox folder** (owner: "let me just import the entire rekordbox folder and let Sounding figure out all the library stuff"). One Mac action, **Import rekordbox Library…**, in File, the Prep menu and Prep's rekordbox Import menu, plus dropping a folder (or a collection XML) on the Prep page. Sounding walks the folder off the main actor and finds, by name only: rekordbox USB or SD exports (`PIONEER/rekordbox/export.pdb` with its ANLZ files and `Contents`), any `DJ_PLAYLISTS` XML, the rekordbox app folder's `share/PIONEER/USBANLZ` (linked to tracks by each ANLZ file's path, then file name and length, then title), and loose music. It never opens `master.db`, its backups, `.edb` or any other `.db`; if those are all there is, the sheet says "Sounding reads rekordbox’s XML export or a rekordbox USB or SD export." with **How to Export…** and stops. With analysis files but no XML, one line says keys and cue points need the XML export. A progress sheet counts tracks, playlists and cues as it reads; a summary shows what was found and how it matched, with **Show Missing Files** and **Allow Access…** for a music folder outside the choice; Import then reuses the XML import's Keep Sounding / Use Imported review. The chosen folder becomes a Music Folder (read-only bookmark, so files stay playable after relaunch); files already in one keep their track. Tracks match by path, then file name, then title, artist and length (your own music first, then SoundCloud). Grids, keys, colours, comments, hot cues, memory cues, loops and phrases go into prep; rekordbox playlists, folders kept as their path, become Prep's rekordbox crates, replaced by path on re-import. Importing the same folder again updates and never duplicates. Ratings and rekordbox waveforms are read but not kept: Sounding has no rating and draws its own waveforms. |

It plays the phone's own `MixEngine` through the shared `DeckControlSurface` and the takeover/hand-back APIs, works signed out, and loads from **Files** first with **SoundCloud Likes** as a second section only when signed in (one `DeckTrackSource` adapter for the coming one Library). Always dark, like Perform. Landscape is the frame: glass strip (Auto Mix headline, bar n/16, step dots, Skip, Auto Mix menu) with round glass Decks View and ••• buttons in one `GlassEffectContainer`; stacked lanes (A solid on top, B outlined); Deck A, compact mixer, Deck B. Each deck: artwork, badge, title, remaining time, key and BPM; CUE (hold), Play, Sync, state chip, Loop (tap toggles, press-and-hold picks 1–16 bars); four pads that are hot cues A–D, or 1/2/4/8 bars while looping. Mixer: per deck one EQ knob (band chosen from its label: High, Mid or Low), the filter and the channel fader, then the crossfader and "Touch any control to take over". Portrait stacks the same pieces and shows all three bands. Grabbing a control turns the strip yellow with "*Control* is yours", Take Over All and Hand Back. SoundCloud on Go: the deck is "Auto Mix only", its transport dimmed, pads replaced by "Go+ unlocks decks" (the iPhone has no jog). **No headphone cue on iPhone**: iOS gives the app one output route and the split-cue path is the Mac's Core Audio device output, so Decks View says so. Owner confirmed 2026-11-09: the Library › Decks row stays; the iPhone no longer drives the Apple TV mixer for now, and that comes back later as a remote mode inside `PhoneDecksView`. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Auto Mix planner (engine, wave 1 lane A).** One planner and one validator: every final plan, fallbacks included, is checked against the simulated source positions (loops, rate, incoming lead). Loop mix is one neutral scored candidate (lean defaults to Sometimes) with lyric-safe loops. **Blend length** setting is Auto / Short / Long: Auto follows the genre (set-learning medians × 0.85, BPM fallback), snapped to 16–48 bars within both runways; Short is 16, Long is 64. **The default blend is the textbook EQ blend**: incoming low killed and mids held back, fader up over 4 bars (2 under 16), a one-beat bass swap on the incoming phrase one with mids restored first, then the outgoing mids out, then its highs and fader over the last 4 bars; a 400 Hz high-pass on a busy incoming. A plain equal fade is never the default. **Fallback ladder** when a blend fails: a short phrase-entered EQ blend, then filter or echo out, then cut; a cut is an echo-out on a phrase line after the energy peak with the incoming on its phrase one, never a play-out-then-cut at the track end. Tempo gaps of 3–6% get a short or filter blend, wider ones echo out. Only the drop opening and vocal hooks in the overlap are protected; leaving from the later drop groove is allowed. Drums-only overlaps skip the key check. |

Offline devices show their tracks as unavailable, not as errors. Rules in §9. |

The Mac Settings window becomes a System Settings-style sidebar with the same tiles, replacing ten toolbar tabs. Table in §3 Settings icons. |

| 2026-11-09 | *(Superseded by the deep and ominous row below.)* **Apple TV sign-in sound, smart-speaker setup style** (owner direction; `Design/specs/chime-signin.md`). It replaces the techno pulse and the "deep, ominous, dripping" idea. The TV beeps once when the sign-in screen appears and plays the handshake chime. Then, while it waits, a quiet sonar ping sounds every 2.6 s (about −23 dBFS). On success it plays a confirmation under 1 s that rises and resolves. The handshake (`ChimeSynth.Style.ripple`) still carries the 128-bit code: rising pentatonic arpeggio waves of bell dyads (C5–E8, 75 ms, 3.96 s) over a soft C add9 pad that enters in rising order, with a gentle stereo shimmer that stays outside the decode band. All sounds are original; never sample or imitate Sonos. The phone also decodes the older dyad layout, so older TVs keep working. |
| 2026-11-09 | *(Superseded by the deep bass row below.)* **Apple TV sign-in sound, deep and ominous** (owner: "i said more deep and omnimous"; `Design/specs/chime-signin.md`). Supersedes the warm, rising smart-speaker row. The handshake (`ChimeSynth.Style.eclipse`) strikes three-note voicings of an A diminished seventh chord (A4–F♯7, 116 ms) over an A1 sub and A2 drone, tritone and minor swells and a distant low bell toll. Success resolves that tension down into a heavy low A minor (1.8 s). The darkness comes from the note set, the register and the bed; the data stays in the decode band and pad energy stays out of it. Waiting (owner: "do it once or twice, and then play ultrasonic sound"): the low toll-and-sonar ping plays twice, then the TV is silent to the ear and only the ultrasonic carrier keeps transmitting. Original synthesis only, never imitate a real product's sounds. The phone still decodes the Ripple and older dyad layouts. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Sign-in pulse: Omen** (owner pick). Ships as `ChimeSynth.shipping = .omen`. |
| 2026-11-09 | *(Superseded by the one-pulse row below.)* **Apple TV sign-in sound, deep bass** (owner references: onderwish's "Deep Dark Bass Slide", two grainy machine bass pulses, a deep minimal UI click; `Design/specs/chime-signin.md`). Supersedes the deep and ominous row. The bass leads: under the handshake (`ChimeSynth.Style.slide`) a wobbling sub slides from E1 to A1, up to E2, down through the tritone and drops to A0. The beep is a short bass slide down, the ping a single deep wobble slide, and success a heavy bass drop that settles (2.4 s). The data stays as soft diminished-chord sines in the decode band, and the bass is cut off below it. Waiting is unchanged: two pings, then only the ultrasonic carrier. Original synthesis only; never sample the references or imitate a real product's sounds. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Apple TV sign-in sound, one deep pulse** (owner: "instead of 10 or so unique dings, i want a sort of 1 sound for handshake, that is a large single sound, an omnious pulse, deep"; `Design/specs/chime-signin.md`). Supersedes the deep bass row. The handshake (`ChimeSynth.Style.omen`) is one 3.7 s sound under one envelope: a driven A1 drone swells in from E1, throbs at 7.1 Hz through a slow phaser, and sinks as it fades. There are no data notes, and no audible run of pitched notes is allowed in any sign-in sound. The 128-bit code rides as a quiet noise-like grain over the pulse (the veil: 16 of 64 slots at 380 Hz–3.85 kHz per 140 ms step, spread across the whole band) and in the ultrasonic carrier. The beep is a deep thump, the ping one throbbing swell played twice, and success one big swell that opens and settles. The phone still decodes Eclipse (Slide), Ripple, Bell and the carrier. Original synthesis only. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Auto Mix: beat-only tracks blend, a real echo out, no audible steps** (owner: "04 is way too hard cut, you can hear it clearly. same with 7 and 11"). A track whose beats are measured but whose downbeats are a guess (no reliable bar phase, or downbeat confidence under 0.8) is phase-locked on the beats with a best-guess bar and only takes an EQ or filter blend of 16 bars or more, which hides a wrong bar; it never cuts, drops in or loops. Only a track with no usable beats, or a pair every blend is vetoed for, echoes out. **The echo out**: a 1-beat delay with feedback starts a bar before the exit on the beat, the incoming enters on its first phrase line at body level with its low killed and a 350 Hz high-pass that opens over a bar, the outgoing fader comes down over 4 bars under the throw, the incoming's lows come back from half a bar in over 2.5 bars while the outgoing's go, and the tail rings 4 bars; it honours your mix-out cue, and when the outgoing is singing it enters on a phrase line with no vocal under the tail. Blends of at least 16 bars (or the genre length, if shorter) are chosen before shorter filter fades, and a blend that runs into the outro counts as leaving from the outro. **Step gate** (offline harness): the summed level may not drop more than 6 dB within 500 ms, and the incoming may not rise more than 9 dB within 250 ms; a transition that fails it counts as a cut. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Auto Mix: fewer audible steps.** The echo out is a 4-bar fade under the throw, with the incoming's lows returning over 2.5 bars, so the room never empties between decks (corpus: echo outs failing the step gate 45 of 71 → 13 of 74). Outside Adventurous, a move that lands the incoming at full on one beat (cut, drop on the one, echo or filter build, loop build) needs a 0.15 lead over the best firing blend. A blend never ends where the incoming drops out (a bar 4 dB under its body within a bar before to 2 bars after the outgoing has gone). The step gate is also read **engine-only**: a fall or rise the dry track makes by itself at the same moment (its own fill or drum break, its beat arriving on the phrase one) isn't counted, as with rule 21. |
| 2026-11-09 | **iPad: one floating main sidebar with the Library sections; Settings keeps its own floating sidebar like the Settings app; no other nested split views** (owner decision, option 1b). Rules in §2 iPad. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Sign-in pulse is Omen, heard alone; code via ultrasonic carrier; code texture only on replay/fallback** (owner: "so i only really want to hear the pulse alone"; `Design/specs/chime-signin.md`). The opening handshake is the Omen pulse with no veil. **Play Sound** and one automatic replay 18 s after the opening (only if no phone has turned up) play the pulse with the veil, and the carrier rises 6 dB while they play. That veil follows the pulse (owner: "make it sound more like part of the pulse"): centred on the 7.1 Hz throb, following its swell, weighted below 1.5 kHz and driven like the bass. Still two audible pings, then the carrier alone. The phone keeps listening for every layer. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Auto Mix looks forward before every mix** (owner: "can this not be handled by doing more looping/effect work/making sure to look forward before a mix?"). The step gate counts every step, the music's own fills and breaks included; the target is 5% of the corpus. Before a mix the planner predicts the summed level from both tracks' bar levels over the handover and 2 bars after, and ranks against a predicted fall of more than 4.5 dB or a rise of the incoming's own music of more than 9 dB; the echo out tries its 3 nearest exit phrase lines and 3 incoming entry lines and keeps the one predicted cleanest. Loops and builds (loop build, filter, echo and reverb builds, loop mix) never start in a drop opening: the first 8 bars after a breakdown, or any bar the sections, the arrangement or the energy profile mark as a drop onset, even when no peak bars are measured. A loop mix never releases into the incoming's dip, as with blends. In the echo out the outgoing's lows start going half a bar after the incoming's start coming back, so the room doesn't thin between them. |

| 2026-11-09 | **One-tap linking between your own devices** (owner, decision 1a). Anything else falls back to the code and **Allow**. The nearby card is remembered: no Link for linked TVs, no Sign In for signed-in TVs, Not Now saved per TV until it's been away and back after 12 hours. | 2026-11-09 | **Search like Apple Music** (owner: "nicer search experience", with iPad Apple Music screenshots; replaces the grouped sections and big Top Result card of 2026-11-08 on iPhone, iPad and Mac; Apple TV is redone separately). **Field:** prompt "Artists, Tracks, Sets and More". **Scope:** SoundCloud or Library (iPhone the system scope bar, iPad a segmented control in the toolbar, Mac `MacGlassTabs` under the toolbar search); Library searches the local library DB and your own files only, sends no request and is instant. **Idle:** Recently Searched with Clear, then Browse Categories; no promo card. Recently Searched stores the result you opened (artist, track, set, playlist or album, with its thumbnail and "Type · Artist" line), never the typed query; 12 at most, newest first, synced with the account settings; long-press to remove; a list on iPhone, an adaptive grid on iPad and Mac; tapping opens or plays it. Browse Categories is a grid (2 columns on iPhone, up to 5 on iPad, 5 on Mac) of 15 genre and mood tiles (`SearchCategory`); a tile opens a category page of SoundCloud's trending chart for that genre, or a track search for moods, one request cached for 30 minutes, with Play and Shuffle. **Typing:** suggestions while typing, then one mixed list of full-width rows (56 pt thumbnail, circle for people; title; "Track · Artist", "Set · Artist", "Album · Owner", "Playlist · Owner", "Artist" or "Profile"; a chevron on artists, playlists and albums, ••• on tracks; tapping a track plays it), Preview marks where they apply. Under the field a row of filter pills: Top Results, Artists, Tracks, Sets, Playlists, Albums, People (Library scope hides kinds it has no matches for). Top Results interleaves the best of every kind after the top result; each other pill filters to that kind and loads more pages on scroll (50 per page, at most 10 pages per query and kind, stops on the first error). Artists are people with tracks; People have none. Loading is a centred `ProgressView`, no results `ContentUnavailableView.search`. Idle, typing and results cross-fade and pills slide (both off under Reduce Motion). Shared: `SearchBrowse.swift`, `SearchComponents.swift`, `UnifiedSearchModel`. |

| 2026-11-09 | **Sign-out revokes with SoundCloud, honestly** (owner brief). One path, `SoundCloudCredentialSource.signOutThisDevice` or `signOutEverywhere`, used by every platform. **Sign Out** on a device whose token is shared (another linked device or peer, an iCloud copy, or any Apple TV or Watch, which only ever hold a transferred token) does a local wipe only and never calls SoundCloud, because revoking would sign the others out. Only a device with a token nobody else holds calls `POST api.soundcloud.com/sign-out` first. **Sign Out of All Devices** always calls `/sign-out` then `/disconnect` (revokes the app's grant), then the existing propagation and wipe. Revocation is best effort, 5 s, never blocks or fails the sign-out; the token travels only in the Authorization header and the Network Activity log shows the call as "Sign out" with no token. Confirmation copy: "Signs you out of SoundCloud in Sounding on all your devices." |

Long tracks and sets scrub as a tape on the shared `SCWaveformRenderer`; click commits. Show Columns replaces Roomy/Dense in TV track lists. Top Shelf and other app-group files are written under `Library/Caches` on tvOS, the only writable part of the shared container there. Rules in §2 Apple TV. |
| 2026-11-09 | **The Mac gets widgets, controls and a Share extension** (brief: "the Mac app embeds no extensions"). Two new targets reuse the iOS widget and Share sources: Now Playing and Recently Played widgets, Play or Pause and Resume controls in Control Center and the menu bar, and a Share extension that hands SoundCloud links to the Mac app instead of the Apple TV, since the Mac plays on its own output. The Mac app now publishes the widget snapshot and handles `soundingtv://widget` links, including the new `/open` link for Play Now, Play Next and Play Last. Share sheets on both platforms use grouped Forms and the orange tint. Widgets stay empty until the Mac app has the `group.red.forbes.sounding` app group. Rule in §2 Mac. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Library pacing** (owner: likes "right away", the full collection "quite quickly", heavy data "one at a time, staggered"). Opened lists walk to the end at once on the user lane at 200 a page and load whole from the database (no Load All once synced); the background walker goes 150 an hour with two requests in flight instead of 90 an hour 6 s apart; background downloads and analysis run one at a time with a 3 to 7 s stagger and pause for Low Power Mode, heat, cellular and streaming. iCloud library sync falls back to local-only for 6 hours when the `LibraryChunk` type is refused. Rule in §9. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Play On holds still and is remembered** (owner: "play on super jumpy, apple tv keeps popping in and out"; "remember plays on if possible and restore after reboot/restart/close"). Root cause: the list showed a linked TV only while Bonjour advertised it or, for the active link, only while the Companion connection was `.connected`; every reconnect (liveness watchdog, backoff retry, foregrounding) tore the browser down and emptied the advertised set, so the TV vanished and came back each cycle. Now one merged, debounced list with hysteresis (10 s settle, 45 s Not Responding, current Output never removed), stable order, and a remembered Output restored within 25 s of launch or quietly dropped back to This *Device*. Rules in §2 All platforms. |
| 2026-11-09 | **An Apple TV that isn't linked asks your iPhone from outside the app** (owner: the TV says "iPhone is nearby" but the phone shows nothing unless Sounding is open). The TV only sees an iPhone while Sounding is open on it, so the TV now asks on its own: when it's open, signed in and either sees an unlinked iPhone of your account or has nothing linked, it saves a `NearbyLinkRequest` to iCloud (at most once per TV every 12 hours), and your iPhone, iPad and Mac show "Living Room is nearby" with **Link** and **Not Now** (Active, with the drawn TV as a picture). Not Now follows the card's rules and is saved to iCloud as a `NearbyLinkSnooze` so the TV stops asking. Withdrawn when the TV links, closes or after 10 minutes. With the app open the card takes over and no banner shows. Subscriptions are now checked against iCloud after every build and daily instead of trusting a local flag, and push registration no longer waits for permission. Needs `NearbyLinkRequest`, `NearbyLinkSnooze`, `SignInRequest` and `AccountSignIn` deployed to the Production schema. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Apple TV banner and library header hold still** (owner: "goes off screen while loading, overlap of the banner and title bar"). The nearby-iPhone and linked toast is a bottom-centre glass capsule inside the safe area, never over the tab bar, never focusable, and the nearby hint hides itself after 10 seconds. The Library page header never grows while syncing: the sync state joins the count line ("23 items · Syncing…"), and a page that shows sync keeps that line reserved so the grid does not shift. Loading placeholders on tvOS pages no longer add their own sync line. Library sidebar rows are 60 pt so all eleven fit, and the sidebar scrolls with focus when they do not. Ellipses are "…", never "...". |
| 2026-11-09 | **Likes stick** (owner bug: a Like "stops being liked after a second"). Cause: the launch Likes fill ran as user work, so its 429 set the global traffic stop, and the Like `PUT` refused by that stop was rolled back silently. The launch fill is now background work; a Like or Unlike blocked by a stop of up to 10 minutes stays pending and retries after the back-off (three tries); a real failure rolls back once with an alert, or the traffic bar when a stop shows. The library database keeps a pending-mutation overlay so a pending or just-confirmed like, unlike, follow or repost wins over refreshes, fills, walks and older peer chunks that started before it (2-minute grace after confirmation). |
| 2026-11-09 | **No more Mac keychain password prompts** (owner: "i see too many of these"). Every keychain item on the Mac moves from the login keychain to the data protection keychain through one helper, `SoundingKeychain`. Existing items move once without losing data, and a refused move is retried rather than replaced. Test, harness and unsigned runs never touch the real login keychain. Rule in §11. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Link a nearby Apple TV from Play On** (owner: "let me connect to a tv via the play on dialog/modal/popover"). The Connect sheet (iPhone, iPad) and the Mac Play On popover list unlinked Apple TVs in a Nearby section; tapping links inline (Connecting… in the row, the code as a sheet on iPhone and iPad and inline on the Mac, Couldn't Connect with Try Again) and makes the TV the Output. Shared `PlayOnNearbyLinking` on the app's running browser. The Mac Now Playing Play On pill menu is unchanged (a menu can't hold a progress row). Rules in §2 All platforms. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Adaptive artwork backgrounds on Apple TV everywhere** (owner: "adaptive artwork backgrounds on tvos too"). Own-file albums and artists move onto the shared column page (`CollectionColumnPage`, extracted from `CollectionHeroPage`) with the drifting backdrop, line art, accent Play and the page mesh; artist and profile pages lose the flat accent wash and the live 80 pt avatar blur for the pre-blurred backdrop and a mesh of the banner's (else the avatar's) colours; the ambient screen drops its live-blurred `AmbientArtworkBackdrop` for Now Playing's own backdrop so the two match. The mesh redraws at 10 fps on Apple TV. Rules in §2 Apple TV and §3 Collection backdrop. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Sonos holds through a mix** (owner: "sonos connection dropped on mix?"). Nothing re-sends the transport mid-mix: `SetAVTransportURI` and `Play` are sent only when a Sonos group is picked, and the mix stays one endless `x-rincon-mp3radio` AAC stream with ICY `StreamTitle` for track changes. Cause: the stream encoder paced itself off the engine tap, re-anchoring its clock on every read, so when the two-deck mix rendered or delivered slower than real time the deficit was forgotten and Sonos's buffer drained; a stall followed by a late burst ran the stream ahead instead. The encoder now follows a wall-clock pacer (`SonosStreamPacer`): it never runs more than 0.25 s ahead, pads encoded silence once it falls 0.3 s behind, drops the oldest audio beyond a 0.75 s backlog so Sonos stays at the live edge, and after a stall over 3 s resyncs instead of bursting. A reconnecting Sonos still gets the last 0.5 s, never the start. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Sonos never goes silent on a failed track.** While Sonos is the Output a track that won't load in the mix engine is no longer handed to the muted AVPlayer (Sonos heard silence). It retries once in the engine after 1 s, then skips to the next track; after 3 failed skips in a row, or with nothing left, playback pauses and the mix engine shows the failure. Elsewhere the AVPlayer fallback is unchanged. Rules in `EngineLoadFailurePolicy`. Playback on Sonos also starts in the engine when Auto Mix and DJ are off. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Output volume is one model, and the volume buttons control a TV or Sonos** (owner: "iphone now playing volume ignored? but play on not ignored"; "let me use phone volume button?"). Cause: while Sonos played, the iPhone stayed the Output, so the Now Playing slider moved the phone's system volume (whose audio is muted while it streams to Sonos), while Play On set the Sonos volume; with a TV the slider was hidden. Now Now Playing, Play On, the Mac slider, iPad ⌘↑/⌘↓ and the Watch share one route and one throttled, echo-proof channel (`Core/Playback/OutputVolume.swift`), and the iPhone and iPad slider shows the TV's volume too (reverses the 2026-11-06 and 2026-11-07 rule that it hides). Locally Play On now sets the system volume like Now Playing. Volume buttons step the TV or Sonos in the foreground with a small glass level capsule; background and lock screen keep the phone's own volume. Rules in §2 All platforms. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Top Shelf, 10/10** (owner: "can we make the top shelf 10/10?"). Square sectioned shelves as in Music: Continue Listening (an unfinished set resumes at its position, with a bar and "48 min left"), Recently Played, Your Likes (from the library database, no request) and Mixes For You. Select and Play both play (owner kept the 2026-11-07 "select plays" rule); Continue Listening resumes. The app saves 800 px art in the app group so 4K is sharp and offline works, and drops the undocumented `t1080x1080` variant the 2x tiles asked for; the extension reads only the snapshot. The shelf refreshes on plays, Likes, pauses and track changes, debounced, only on real changes. Signed out or empty is the flat photo, not line art. Rules in §2 Apple TV. |
| 2026-11-09 | **TV sign-in spacing** (owner: countdown "spaced weird"). New code in Ns has a reserved, centred 28 pt caption below the status, with monospaced digits and explicit 32/24/8/16 pt stack spacing. Supersedes the countdown appended to the status sentence. Rule in §2 Apple TV. |
| 2026-11-09 | **TV sign-in background has quiet depth** (owner: "background graphic is quite boring"). Copper-and-amber radial washes drift and breathe behind three jack contours with a soft shadow and small float; rainbow arcs go. Core Animation only, 30 fps cap, still under Reduce Motion, while connecting and when inactive. Rule in §2 Apple TV; this sign-in composition is an exception to §4’s four-to-six contour vocabulary. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Play Sound feedback is one halo** (owner: "weird and ugly and not apple like"). A gentle spring expands a soft orange halo from the QR plate, then fades; the plate and QR code stay still. Reduce Motion uses opacity only. Replaces beat-driven rings, cable pulses and plate pumping. Rule in §2 Apple TV. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Warm, short sign-in chime** (owner: "the sound is horrifying"; now "pleasant, Apple-like"). Original C4–E4–G4, 1.45 s, 35 ms soft attack, warm sine-based timbre, −16 dBFS peak; success E4–G4–C5, 1.15 s. Supersedes all Omen/ominous/bass/veil choices and the ban on pitched notes. Only the existing ultrasonic carrier transmits the code; QR and numeric fallback remain. Audio-thread callbacks are nonisolated. Rule in §2 Apple TV. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Restore the nearby card’s Apple TV symbol** (owner: "i prefered the old image of an apple tv"). Restore the large 92 pt hierarchical `appletv.fill` via `DeviceClass.symbolName`, reversing a4164289 for the card. Keep `DeviceIllustration` in the discovery header, where it is still used. Rule in §9 Nearby Apple TV card. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Centre numeric link codes** (owner: 000-000 alignment "is weird"). One monospaced text run, baseline hyphen, equal thin spaces, no trailing tracking or digit boxes; centre the surrounding instructions and put alert codes on their own line. Rule in §2 Apple TV. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Connecting replaces QR during a link** (owner: QR shows while the phone is already linking). A server-owned progress state spans offer exchange, credential handoff and authenticated connection, with a device-named centred spinner. Companion credential adoption also publishes progress. Only failure/timeout restores sign-in choices; required numeric approval remains actionable, same-owner linking never flashes its code. Rule in §2 Apple TV. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Home hero full bleed, adaptive artist pages, orange Search glyph, page links** (owner, on the iPad: "that image should go off to the top of the page… it shouldn't just cap underneath the word home"; "have a look at… an artist… improve how the dynamic or adaptive backgrounds work"; "search icon white"). The Continue From hero's backdrop moves out of the hero into one layer behind Home's scroll view (`collectionScrollBackdrop`, shared with album pages), so it runs to the top of the window under the status bar, bar and large title on iPhone and iPad, and under the toolbar and title row on the Mac, with a softer 104 pt foot. Artist and profile pages on iPhone, iPad and Mac drop the flat accent wash, the in-row hero image and the Mac's own backdrop for the shared `collectionPersonBackdrop` (full bleed, extended under the sidebar, banner or pre-blurred drifting avatar, measured name scrim) and `collectionPersonPage` (banner or avatar mesh, always dark). Page colours are picked vivid (grey cells lean to the most vivid cell, a 45% saturation floor, the bottom edge weighted to its most vivid cell, two deeper mesh corners). The iPad Search item gets the orange `SidebarTabIcon`. `soundingtv://open-page?url=` navigates to a SoundCloud artist, playlist, album or track page for screenshot drives. Rules in §2 All platforms, §3 Collection backdrop, §9 Continue from and §12. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Instant play** (owner: "sometimes there's a 1 or 2 second delay before the music starts … there should be an instant play"). First audio never waits on the full tracklist, analysis, lyrics, comments or the mix engine; collections start from what the device has and fill the queue after audio starts; touch-down, hover and focus prewarm the first stream; local bytes start with `playImmediately`. Anything played in full is cached encrypted in Caches for at least 3 hours (1.5 GB soft, 2 GB hard, 72 h max). Playing from a cover opens Now Playing straight away with the cover zooming into the artwork (iPhone and iPad `.zoom`, Mac flight), a haptic on iPhone and a spinner-then-Pause on the hero button. Rules in §5, §6 and §9. |
| 2026-11-09 | **Auto Mix: blends, loops and filter fades first; echo outs about 1 in 5 at most** (owner decision "1a"). The 5% step-gate target is met by building the missing moves, never by falling back to echo outs, and echo outs should be about 20% of transitions or fewer. Analysis keeps a level for every beat as well as every bar, so the planner sees fills and gaps shorter than a bar (tracks analysed before this keep the bar levels until they are analysed again). A blend whose incoming falls more than 6 dB from one beat to the next as the outgoing leaves is refused, and a plan whose predicted summed level falls more than 6.5 dB, or whose incoming jumps more than 10 dB, is refused rather than ranked down. When the incoming dips at the handover, the blend holds the outgoing 2 or 4 bars longer, past the dip, with the bass swap where it was. A blend can also start the incoming on its groove (where its bass arrives) and swap the bass a phrase later, so the incoming's own arrival happens under a low fader. The look-ahead reads the incoming 8 bars past the handover and ranks down a mix that drops it into its own breakdown there. An EQ blend may enter under the tail of an incoming vocal line that ends within its first 4 bars (fader low, mids held back); two vocals still never overlap. A beat-only track's phrase lines are counted from its strongest section. Two tracks whose beats can't be placed, only their tempo, take a 4-bar high-pass handover: the outgoing is thinned to 600 Hz over the first bar so the kicks never flam, then the incoming takes over. Filter fades bring the incoming's lows back over 4 beats starting half a beat before the swap, high-pass handovers over a bar, and the echo out drops the outgoing's lows on a power-linear ramp while the incoming's return on the smooth curve, so the lows never swell; the echo throw never starts from a quiet bar, and when the outgoing's last vocal runs late the echo out still uses a phrase line before it rather than a cut at the end of the track. The offline harness now builds beat-only grids as the app does. |
| 2026-11-07 | When the Output is an Apple TV, the Mac, iPhone or iPad driving it analyses the next 5 queued tracks it has downloaded (background, one at a time) and sends its stored Music Analysis for them to the TV over Companion, so the TV blends on measured bars instead of its own tracker. No SoundCloud requests; tracks not downloaded there are still left to the TV. |
| 2026-11-12 | **Every feature on every device** (owner: "these things MUST be there for all devices, not be device specific"). Caching, performance and data work go in shared code and reach iPhone, iPad, Mac, Apple TV and Watch together; iOS 27-only APIs keep an iOS 26 path. Rule in §11. |
| 2026-11-12 | **Configurable and bigger widgets, Watch Smart Stack relevance and double tap, Watch controls, CarPlay mini player and compact activity** (owner). New **Play** widget (pick a playlist, setlist or station; small to extra large), large and extra large Now Playing and Recently Played, a Next Track control on iPhone, iPad, Mac and Watch, an Identify Song control on the Watch, the Watch Listening Smart Stack widget (headphones, workout), double tap on the Watch Play and Pause button, CarPlay `allowsMiniPlayer` behind `#available(iOS 27)`, and `supplementalActivityFamilies([.small, .medium])` on the Now Playing Live Activity. Rules in §2 iPhone, Mac, Apple Watch and CarPlay. |
| 2026-11-12 | **Translate lyrics, controller lights, network MIDI** (owner-approved). Lyrics get a Translate button in the pane (iPhone, iPad, Mac) that translates each synced line on the device with Apple's Translation framework, keeping its timing; LRCLIB stays the only lyrics source; Apple TV and Watch have no Translation framework (§5). Supported controllers get output feedback: play, cue, sync, loop and hot-cue lights and the channel meters, sent through each profile's light table and throttled (lights on change, meters and other continuous feedback at most 15 a second, at most 24 messages per pass), dark on decks the controller can't drive. Network MIDI: the iPad can be a network MIDI session and connect to hosts; the Mac lists the sessions set up in Audio MIDI Setup. Controllers work on iPad Decks too (`dj-controllers.md`). |
| 2026-11-12 | **Siri, Focus, Visual Intelligence and the Mac Dock** (owner-approved). Siri, Spotlight and Shortcuts play results show an interactive Now Playing snippet (`SnippetIntent`) on iPhone, iPad and Mac: artwork, title, artist, "Playing on <Output>", and icon buttons Like, Skip and Add to Playlist (your own playlists, picked in a follow-up snippet, then "Added to <Playlist>."); "Now Playing" is its own intent. A Sounding **Focus filter** (Settings › Focus, iPhone, iPad and Mac) sets the Output, **Quiet Auto Mix** (energy 3, no Surprises or risers), **Wind Down** over 1–120 minutes and **Workout Energy** (heart rate where there is one, else energy 8); Focus overrides live in memory and never change synced settings, and the Output moves back when the Focus ends if it still holds the Focus choice. **Visual Intelligence** on iPhone (and Mac where the system offers it) reads the text in view on device and finds the artist or track on SoundCloud: one artist and one track search, cached 10 minutes, cache only while SoundCloud is limiting us. The Mac **Dock menu** shows now playing, Play/Pause, Next, Previous, Like and five recent tracks; a small **AppleScript** dictionary adds play, pause, playpause, next track, previous track and read-only player state, current track name, artist and URL. The Playback menu, Dock and scripts share one transport. |
| 2026-11-12 | **Outside alerts fire, one per TV, and clear themselves** (owner: a pile of "Apple TV wants to sign in" on the iPhone; "can we wipe these after they are no longer relevant?"). The "is nearby" and "New Sign-In" subscriptions had never saved: CloudKit allows five fields per notification and they asked for seven. Each alert now asks for five at most (tested), and the app reads the full record before it acts. Sign-in requests collapse per TV, a new one withdraws the last, the start screen asks at most every 10 minutes, and delivered alerts are swept when they no longer apply (§8 Notifications outside the app). |
| 2026-11-12 | **TV sign-in by account state** (owner answers Q1 to Q10 in `Design/specs/tv-signin-flows.md`). Q1: auto-link with no taps only when this device was already linked to the TV and never unlinked it, on the same Apple ID and SoundCloud account ("link auto if already linked and not unlinked"); an explicit unlink blocks it until you link again; this replaces decision 1a, so a first link on the same Apple ID shows the six digits and **Allow**. Q2: the TV never signs a phone in; same Apple ID gets **Continue as Sam** from iCloud. Q3: switching a TV's account is confirmed on the phone and on the TV, and the pair is sent only after the TV accepts. Q4: no nearby card or notification for a TV on another account. Q5: such a TV is in the other-devices bar only as the Output. Q6: Add a Device on a signed-in TV is control-only. Q7: iCloud sessions per account, guest sign-ins off the owner's iCloud. Q8: a switch clears the old account's data like Sign Out, Links kept. Q9: "Signed in as Alex." on the TV's done screen, "Sign it in to Sounding as Alex." on the card. Q10: the Now Playing bar never names the TV's account. Rules in §2, §8 and §9; the per-account session record needs the CloudKit Production schema deployed. |
| 2026-11-12 | **The TV names a device only when it knows it** (owner, from the real Apple TV: "Link a device modal on TV should not say iPhone/iPad if it does not know, it could also be a Mac"). The sign-in and link UI uses the peer's `DeviceClass` (hello, offer identity or nearby advert) or its own name when known, and neutral wording otherwise: "Sign in with Another Device", "your device", "Finish on your device.", "A device", "Scan with your phone's camera" only for the camera. One pure place: `PeerDevice` and `TVPairingCopy`. No new wire fields. §8. |

The "Apple TV is waiting to sign in" row is gone from iPhone and iPad Home; the nearby Apple TV card is the only prompt. The "Sign in again to refresh your picks." row is replaced by a **Reconnect SoundCloud** card in the same bottom-rising style (sheet on the Mac), shown once per launch or when the status flips to Needs Reconnect, with **Reconnect** and **Not Now**; the nearby card wins when both are due. `bottomCard` is the shared container for both. Rules in §2 and §9. |
| 2026-11-12 | **Apple TV layout and focus fixes** (owner: "issues with the padding at top and also glitching when switching tab bar items, keeps glitching content up/down, going text black on hover", and Library › History "too awful"). Now Playing starts below the tab bar like every tab; the idle artwork only appears while focus is on the page; one tab bar that fits (Search and Settings as icons); no app animation on tab switches; an opaque dark backdrop behind every tab; selected Now Playing panel tabs keep a white label (black only on the focused white fill); History and Following/Followers search on demand; the Library sidebar is 400 pt. |

The two tracks loaded on the mix engine's decks get the full Music Analysis on the TV (BPM, beats, key, energy), from the deck's own audio with no extra SoundCloud traffic, while playing, one job at a time at a priority that can't cause dropouts. Still no background backlog on TV. Readouts never show an estimate's energy, and say "analysing" while it runs. Rules in §8 (BPM and key words) and §9 (Tracks on the decks). |

One page grid for every TV page and cover (80 pt sides, safe top + 24, 48 from the bottom; backdrops as backgrounds, no `GeometryReader` wrapping a page, no fixed text sizes), which removes the black band under Now Playing. Now Playing follows the mockup: full-width tape with tracklist titles above and chapter and comment dots below, 84 pt Up Next rows, one source line style. Idle hides the tab bar, centres the artwork and its caption and puts the clock on the grid. The Settings tab is a titled tab with the gear, because tvOS drops icon-only tab labels. Decks stats show only real values with "~" for estimates and the Apple TV analysis line. Behind the Track, Comments, Settings, Stations and History take the same grid, and empty states are centred. Rules in §2 Apple TV. |

Suggestion captions are plain English on every device (`TrackMapCopy.suggestionCaption`), and the panel row that opens the page is "All Cue Points". Rules in §2 Apple TV and §5. |

The Mac gains Cue Points in Now Playing. Rules in §5. |

Now Playing respects the top safe area and starts `max(96, safe top + 24)` from the screen top, so with the bar visible the content sits 24 pt under it and settles to the mockup position when the bar hides (smooth, 0.45 s). The backdrop is the lightly blurred artwork with the mockup scrim, no palette blobs; the same cover backdrop on Cue Points, Comments and Setlist. Tape labels truncate with an ellipsis in the room to the next tick. Rules in §2 Apple TV and §5 Background. |

The cue store announces every change by track; open cue screens (Mac Prep, Mac Now Playing, iPhone and iPad sheet, Apple TV page and panel) reload when another screen, iCloud sync, a rekordbox import or Auto Mix changes their track, keeping selection and in-progress edits. Undo is reachable on iPhone, iPad and Apple TV, not just the Mac. Rules in §5. |
| 2026-11-12 | **Mac sidebar like Apple Music; Decks is Perform; Prep in the Library; Plan over Perform** (owner: "can we improve the sidebar", the Perform / Prep / Plan capsules are "fuck ugly", "decks should just be perform… prep goes into library somehow… plan… doesn't even damn load"). Decks loses its mode switch. Prep is a Library page in the window's appearance plus a Get Info-style inspector from any track's context menu, and the deck's Edit in Prep opens the Prep window. Plan is a sheet over Perform (⌘3). Two bugs behind the owner's complaints: Prep read its likes from the legacy `likes_index` table that only peer sync fills, so it showed "No Tracks to Prepare" against hundreds of likes (now the Library database); Plan built full planning features for every queued track one after another before showing anything (now the next ten, concurrently). §2 Mac. |

Settings › Library › Prep › **Appearance**: **Match Window** (default, the window's light or dark) or **Dark** (the Prep page, the Prep inspector and the Prep window stay dark whatever the window is). Device-local (`PrepAppearance`, `SettingsStore.prepAppearance`), applied live. §2 Mac. |
| 2026-11-12 | **A mix is long, or says so in its title** (owner, 1a: label EP uploads lost Lyrics because their description says "Tracklist:"). `DeckIdentityResolver.isLikelyMix`: 20 minutes or longer, or the title names a DJ set, mixtape, mix session, guest mix or radio show; the description only counts on tracks of 10 minutes or more, and the word "tracklist" alone never makes a mix. Applies on every device. |
